### [1] [Search for new physics using single-lepton events with high multiplicities of jets and b jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09567)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据中，利用单轻子+多喷注（含b喷注）末态，无缺能要求，搜寻R宇称破缺超对称模型；采用大半径喷注质量和作为区分变量，未见显著超出，排除质量低于1890 GeV的此类胶球子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测R宇称破缺的超对称性（特别是baryon-number violating耦合），弥补传统缺能搜寻的盲区，拓展对强子型超对称末态的敏感度。

Method: 基于CMS探测器采集的138 fb⁻¹ 13 TeV pp碰撞数据，构建单轻子、高喷注数（含b-tagged jets）、零/低E_T^miss事件样本；以大半径喷注（large-radius jets）的质量和（sum of large-radius jet masses）为关键区分变量，结合背景建模（主要为t-tbar, W+jets等）与统计推断（CLs方法）。

Result: 观测数据与标准模型背景预测一致，无统计显著性超出（p-value > 0.05）；在95%置信水平下，排除胶球子质量 < 1890 GeV 的RPV SUSY信号模型（gluino → tbs衰变链）。

Conclusion: 该分析对R宇称破缺超对称模型中胶球子对产生及三夸克强子衰变提供了迄今最强的直接限制，凸显了无缺能多喷注末态在BSM探测中的互补价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a search for beyond the standard model physics using single-lepton events with a high multiplicity of jets, including those identified as bottom quark jets, without a requirement on missing transverse momentum. The analysis is based on proton-proton collision data collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. This search is sensitive to $R$-parity violating supersymmetry models, where supersymmetric particles can decay into standard-model particles through interactions that violate baryon number conservation. In particular, the signal model considered is gluino pair production, where each gluino decays into top, bottom, and strange quarks. The sum of large-radius jet masses is used to distinguish the signal from background, as it effectively captures the features of high jet multiplicity and high interaction energy. No significant excess of data over the background predictions is observed. Gluinos in this model have been excluded for masses below 1890 GeV at 95% confidence level.

</details>


### [2] [Atmospheric Neutrino Oscillations: the Full Picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09714)
*Philipp Eller*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次联合分析了来自Super-Kamiokande、IceCube-DeepCore、KM3NeT/ORCA的大气中微子数据以及Daya Bay的反应堆中微子数据，构建统一物理模型，在无显著参数张力下给出具有竞争力的中微子混合参数测量，排除CP守恒，并强烈支持中微子质量谱的正规序（Normal Ordering）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破实验合作组壁垒，实现跨实验、多探测器、多源中微子数据的联合振荡分析，以提升参数约束能力并检验标准三味振荡框架的一致性

Method: 构建统一的三味中微子振荡物理模型，联合拟合Super-Kamiokande、IceCube-DeepCore、KM3NeT/ORCA大气中微子数据与Daya Bay反应堆中微子数据，共839,048个事件、1536个观测bin，含91个自由参数（含振幅、能量刻度、系统误差等）

Result: 获得高精度θ23、Δm²₃₁、δCP等参数测量；Δχ²(CP-conserving vs. CP-violating) > 9；Δχ²(IO vs. NO) ≈ 12，显著偏好正规序；各实验间无显著拉格朗日乘子偏移或后验张力

Conclusion: 多实验联合振荡分析在技术上可行且物理上稳健，为未来JUNO、DUNE与Hyper-K等实验的全局拟合奠定方法论基础，并强化了轻子CP破坏与正规质量序的实验证据

Abstract: We present the first combined oscillation analysis of multiple atmospheric neutrino datasets, featuring data from Super-Kamiokande, IceCube-DeepCore, and KM3NeT/ORCA together with reactor data from Daya Bay. Such combinations have long been considered infeasible outside experimental collaborations; we demonstrate that a unified physics model can simultaneously describe all datasets with no significant parameter tensions. Fitting 839\,048 events across 1536 bins with 91 parameters, our combined analysis yields competitive measurements of the neutrino mixing parameters, disfavors CP conservation, and prefers the Normal over the Inverted Mass Ordering.

</details>

### [3] [A Landscape of Cosmological Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07663)
*S. Shajidul Haque,Bret Underwood*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了早期宇宙中量子到经典转变的统一框架，通过分析混合态的纯度与动量方差，揭示了退相干模型的几何结构，并利用引力势衰减模对退相干机制施加严格的理论约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前观测要求原初扰动是绝热、近高斯、近尺度不变的，但满足这些条件的混合态仍存在未被观测约束的自由度（如纯度和动量方差），需系统刻画其物理后果与模型限制。

Method: 基于Glauber-Sudarshan P-函数的正定性条件，结合辐射主导时期引力势衰减模的动力学演化，分析动量方差如何源生该衰减模，并据此推导非线性引力效应所施加的理论边界。

Result: 发现环境必须主动注入动量（而非抑制）才能使P-函数正定；该增强的动量方差激发辐射时代的引力势衰减模；该模的初始振幅导致非线性引力约束，排除退相干热态，并将振幅对角型退相干模型限制在少于70 e-倍涨落内。

Conclusion: 构建了一个统一的几何框架，用以评估早期宇宙量子态的退相干路径及其可观测后果，明确了不同指针基与退相干机制在量子-经典过渡中的可容许参数空间。

Abstract: Current observations constrain primordial perturbations to be adiabatic, approximately Gaussian, and nearly-scale invariant. However, a generic mixed state satisfying these constraints has additional unconstrained degrees of freedom, which can be parameterized by the purity of the state and its momentum variance. This allowable parameter space reveals a unified geometric landscape of mixed states, allowing us to map and relate distinct models of decoherence and their respective pointer bases. Crossing the threshold of a regular, positive-definite Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-function requires the environment to actively inject momentum into the system, rather than suppress it. This enhanced momentum variance dynamically sources the decaying mode of the gravitational potential in the radiation era. While the decaying mode vanishes fast enough to preserve the temporal coherence of the Cosmic Microwave Background, its initial amplitude places severe theoretical constraints on decoherence models to avoid gravitational non-linearities. These non-linearity bounds definitively rule out decohered thermal states, while long-wavelength divergences restrict amplitude-diagonal decoherence models to fewer than 70 $e$-folds of inflation. Altogether, we present a unifying framework for evaluating the quantum-to-classical transition of the early universe.

</details>


### [4] [Spacetime Bartnik Mass Positivity and Temporal Monotonicity for Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07781)
*Lars Andersson,Marcus Khuri,Marc Mars,Walter Simon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文定义了一种Bartnik型的准局域质量，并证明了其在两类黑洞相关区域上的正性与时间单调性：对于具有表观视界边界的紧致类空超曲面，或具有渐近平直端且在任意容许延拓中包含表观视界的非紧致类空超曲面，该质量严格为正；在相应的时间演化情景中，该质量关于时间非减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立具有良好几何与物理意义的准局域质量定义，以刻画黑洞区域的能量含量，并验证其基本性质（如正性与演化单调性）以支持广义相对论中的能量守恒与黑洞热力学类比。

Method: 基于Bartnik质量框架，结合外推延拓（admissible extensions）、最小面积封闭面（即表观视界）约束及ADM质量比较技术，在广义相对论的初值数据集上构造并分析该准局域质量泛函。

Result: 1) 对含表观视界边界的紧致或渐近平直非紧致类空超曲面，所定义的准局域质量严格大于零；2) 在满足主能量条件与适当演化方程的时空中，该质量沿类时方向非减。

Conclusion: 该Bartnik型准局域质量提供了黑洞区域能量的合理度量，其正性与时间单调性分别对应于引力正能量定理与黑洞面积定理的准局域推广，强化了准局域质量在数学广义相对论中的基础地位。

Abstract: We define a quasilocal mass of Bartnik type, and establish its positivity and temporal monotonicity properties for two classes of domains associated with black holes. More precisely, we first show that the quasilocal mass is strictly positive for spacelike hypersurfaces that are: compact with apparent horizon boundary or noncompact with asymptotically flat ends and containing an apparent horizon in any admissible extension. Secondly, we show that the quasilocal mass is monotonically nondecreasing in time within evolutionary scenarios related to the two aforementioned settings.

</details>


### [5] [Does Eternal Inflation Violate the Smeared Null Energy Condition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08003)
*Dong-Hui Yu,Yong Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨永恒暴胀中量子涨落引发的哈勃参数上升是否违反涂抹零能量条件（SNEC），结论是：在半经典慢滚框架下，随机扩散驱动永恒暴胀，但不会导致SNEC违背；因引力反作用在SNEC极限被达到前早已破坏背景时空假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永恒暴胀中 inflaton 场的随机向上量子涨落会局部增强哈勃参数，看似与涂抹零能量条件（SNEC）——一个沿类光测地线对负能量密度的半局域约束——相冲突，需厘清该张力是否构成真实违背。

Method: 采用Fokker-Planck方程分析哈勃参数的系综漂移行为，并辅以单轨迹分析，揭示SNEC特征e-folding数 N_SNEC 与布朗涨落特征e-folding数 N_BR 之间存在强时间尺度分离。

Result: 哈勃参数的系综漂移受慢滚参数和半经典压制因子参数化限制；且 N_SNEC ≫ N_BR，表明引力反作用在SNEC数学极限可达之前即已使背景时空近似失效。

Conclusion: 标准随机扩散机制可驱动永恒暴胀，但在半经典慢滚 regime 内不导致SNEC违背；SNEC 与永恒暴胀在该框架下相容。

Abstract: The smeared null energy condition (SNEC) imposes a semilocal bound on the negative energy accumulated along null geodesics. In eternal inflation, rare stochastic upward fluctuations of the inflaton locally increase the Hubble parameter, creating an apparent tension with the SNEC. Focusing on a canonical single-field model, we investigate whether this quantum-induced self-reproduction violates the SNEC. Using the Fokker-Planck equation, we demonstrate that the ensemble drift of the Hubble parameter is parametrically bounded by slow-roll parameters and semiclassical suppression. Furthermore, a complementary single-trajectory analysis reveals a strong timescale hierarchy, $N_{\rm SNEC} \gg N_{\rm BR}$. This indicates that even for rare upward stochastic excursions, gravitational backreaction invalidates the background spacetime assumption long before the SNEC bound can be mathematically approached. We conclude that while standard stochastic diffusion drives eternal inflation, it does not inherently lead to SNEC violations within the semiclassical slow-roll regime.

</details>


### [6] [Degenerate Geometries as Matter-Free Physical Configurations in General Relativity: Three Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08022)
*Juri Dimaschko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种通过分支坐标变换从Rindler、Minkowski和Schwarzschild真空度规导出的退化时空构型（具有虫洞拓扑），证明它们在Einstein–Palatini–Cartan框架下无物质源（即无 exotic matter），且无法由非退化时空连续极限得到，表明退化几何构成广义相对论构型空间中的一个独立扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否存在无需奇异物态支撑的、具有虫洞拓扑的真空解，挑战传统薄壳模型中‘虫洞必含奇异物质’的结论，并厘清退化几何在广义相对论理论结构中的地位。

Method: 采用Einstein–Palatini–Cartan变分形式（即将联络与度规作为独立场变量），对三类分支坐标诱导的退化度规（Rindler型平面喉、Klinkhamer型球对称喉、Schwarzschild-Klinkhamer型球对称喉）进行场方程分析，检验其能量动量张量是否严格为零，并考察其与对应非退化解之间的连续性。

Result: 三类退化虫洞构型均满足真空Einstein–Palatini–Cartan方程（T_μν ≡ 0），不依赖奇异物质；其物理表现涵盖纯拓扑结构与真实引力效应；且不存在从常规非退化时空到这些退化构型的光滑极限过程。

Conclusion: 退化虫洞是广义相对论中一类自洽、无物质源、拓扑非平凡的真空解，构成独立于常规洛伦兹流形的构型子空间，提示引力理论的几何自由度可超越标准度规/联络相容性约束。

Abstract: We examine three degenerate spacetime configurations with wormhole topology, obtained via branching coordinate transformations of the Rindler, Minkowski, and Schwarzschild vacuum metrics.These configurations are, respectively, a Rindler wormhole with a planar throat, and the Klinkhamer and Schwarzschild Klinkhamer wormholes with spherical throats. Within the framework of the Einstein Palatini Cartan formulation, we demonstrate that these degenerate configurations are matter free. In this regard, they differ fundamentally from their nondegenerate wormhole counterparts in the thin shell model, which require exotic matter. Nevertheless, the degenerate configurations considered here exhibit distinct physical manifestations, ranging from purely topological structures to genuine gravitational effects. Furthermore, in all three examples, we demonstrate the absence of a limiting transition from the non-degenerate spacetime to the matter free degenerate configuration. This suggests that degenerate geometries constitute an independent sector of the configuration space of general relativity.

</details>

*Jack C. M. Hughes,Joudy F. Jamal Beek,Fedor V. Kusmartsev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维伪黎曼流形上的曲率张量在2-形式空间上的手征矩阵表示，特别关注爱因斯坦条件与翘曲积结构（如1+3、2+2、3+1分解）之间的代数关系；通过构造不同分解下的6×6曲率矩阵及其相似变换，给出爱因斯坦翘曲积的代数分类，并结合Petrov类型与共形几何得出其类型限制及闭流形上的拓扑约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解爱因斯坦条件在翘曲积时空中的代数实现，统一处理不同签名（1+3, 2+2, 3+1）下曲率张量的手征结构与Petrov分类的关系，并探究半共形平坦极限下的刚性结果。

Method: 利用2-形式空间的Hodge分解与翘曲积诱导的外代数直和分解，构建曲率张量的6×6手征矩阵表示；推导基/纤维分解下的替代矩阵表示；显式计算三类翘曲积（1+3, 2+2, 3+1）对应的矩阵及联系二者的相似变换；结合Weyl张量的自对偶/反对偶部分分析Petrov类型，并应用Atiyah–Singer指标定理与Weitzenböck恒等式讨论闭黎曼情形的拓扑限制。

Result: （1）四维伪黎曼流形为爱因斯坦当且仅当曲率矩阵的非对角3×3块为零；（2）对三类翘曲积，给出显式矩阵表示与相似变换；（3）对应Petrov类型分别为：3+1型—一般为I型，2+2型—D型，1+3型—必为O型（即共形平坦）；（4）在闭黎曼、半共形平坦假设下，所有此类爱因斯坦翘曲积必平坦。

Conclusion: 翘曲积结构与爱因斯坦条件在曲率张量的手征矩阵表示中体现为严格的分块对角化与相似等价性；Petrov分类由翘曲分解的维度配对完全决定；半共形平坦性在紧致情形下强制整体平坦性，揭示了代数条件、共形几何与拓扑之间的深刻耦合。

Abstract: On four-dimensional (pseudo-)Riemannian manifolds $\mathcal{M}$ the curvature tensor (viewed as an endomorphism on 2-forms) admits a chiral $6 \times 6$ matrix representation which decomposes into four $3 \times 3$ blocks. $\mathcal{M}$ is Einstein if and only if the off-diagonal blocks vanish. If the manifold is a warped product $\mathcal{M} = F \times_f B$, then there exists an alternative matrix representation relative to the decomposition of the 2-forms into spaces induced by the exterior algebra on both the base and the fiber. These two representations are not independent and a similarity transformation can be found between them. We construct these matrices and associated transformations for $1+3$, $2+2$ and $3+1$ warped products, giving classifications for the Einstein limits from this algebraic perspective. Using this, one can easily Petrov classify the Einstein warped products for each case considered: $3+1$ are generically type-\RNum{1}, $2+2$ are type-D while $1+3$ are constrained to be type-O. In the closed Riemannian case, there are a number of topological restrictions on these manifolds that we discuss: in the half-conformally flat limit, each of these Einstein warped products must be flat.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力效应对黑洞坍缩动力学及热力学相结构的影响，特别是宇宙学常数与量子修正的协同作用。

Method: 基于高维量子Oppenheimer-Snyder模型，引入负宇宙学常数（AdS背景），在扩展相空间（将宇宙学常数视为热力学压强）框架下计算量子修正的温度、熵与热容等热力学量。

Result: 量子修正使小AdS黑洞温度趋于零而非发散；热容曲线出现额外拐点，表明存在由量子效应驱动的新型热力学相变；该行为显著区别于经典AdS黑洞。

Conclusion: 量子效应不仅正则化黑洞奇点附近的发散行为，还实质性地重构其热力学相图，证实量子引力在AdS黑洞微观结构演化中起决定性作用。

Abstract: We extended the higher-dimensional quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder model to the case with a cosmological constant. For AdS case, we discuss its thermodynamic properties in extend phase space formalism and make comparison with classical black holes. For quantum-corrected small black holes in AdS spacetime, the temperature no longer diverges but tends to zero. Additionally, the heat capacity exhibits characteristic behavior indicative of an extra phase transition induced by quantum corrections, highlighting the profound impact of quantum effects on black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [9] [Non conservative conformal Killing gravity: coupling the dark sector with curvature and matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08213)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Carlo Alberto Mantica,Luca Guido Molinari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Harada引力理论（含非守恒能量-动量张量），将其推广为共形Killing扩展的Rastall引力，包含Unimodular引力作为特例；在FRW宇宙学背景下，结合CMB观测约束，导出暗 sector与物质仅通过能量-动量张量迹耦合的具体形式，并给出修正的Friedmann与连续性方程，其中尘埃能量密度标度演化指数为-3/(1+τ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能自然容纳暗物质与暗能量并实现其与普通物质耦合的修改引力理论，同时兼容CMB观测约束。

Method: 将Harada引力表述为爱因斯坦方程附加一个散度为零的共形Killing张量与一个正比于度规、线性依赖标量曲率及能量-动量张量迹的张量项；在FRW背景中施加CMB参数限制，并推导宇宙学演化方程。

Result: 获得暗 sector张量的具体表达式；导出修正的Friedmann方程与连续性方程；证明尘埃与暗流体总能量-动量张量守恒；尘埃能量密度满足ρ ∝ a^{-3/(1+τ)}演化律。

Conclusion: Harada引力的共形Killing扩展提供了一个自洽框架，使暗 sector 与普通物质通过迹耦合，且在CMB约束下可导出可观测的宇宙学行为，统一涵盖Rastall与Unimodular引力。

Abstract: The so called Harada gravity with non conserved energy-momentum tensor is here taken into account. It includes Rastall gravity as a special case. The field equations are written as Einstein equations where the source is supplemented by a divergence-free conformal Killing tensor and a tensor proportional to the metric, linear in the scalar curvature and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. These terms can be natural candidates for dark sector and give rise to a coupling of the dark sector with the matter content. This is a convex but infinite-dimensional problem. Abstract: Using the open quantum systems formalism, we study the propagation of a quark-antiquark pair propagating through a dense QCD plasma of size $L$ and transverse momentum broadening transport coefficient $\hat q$, and we derive the Lindblad evolution equation for the density matrix of the system. We focus on the boosted regime where the opening angle $θ_{q\bar q}$ of this effective colour dipole satisfies $θ_{q\bar q}\ll 1$. In the correlation limit where the quark-antiquark relative transverse momentum $p_\perp$ is much larger than the imbalance $q_\perp$ as well as the medium typical transverse momentum scale $Q_s=\sqrt{\hat q L}$, we demonstrate that the resulting Wigner distribution displays quasi factorisation between a hard factor describing the hard splitting producing the $q\bar q$ pair and the transverse momentum imbalance $q_\perp$-distribution encoding the broadening induced by the medium. The factorisation is violated by a "colour decoherence" factor that controls the $θ_{q\bar q}$ dependence of the $q_\perp$-distribution through the ratio $θ_{q\bar q}/θ_c$, with $θ_c \sim (\hat q L^3)^{-1/2}$. The open quantum systems approach enables us to clarify the role of this critical angle $θ_c$ and its associated critical time $t_c$ in the genuine quantum decoherence of the density matrix in colour and kinematic space: in particular, $t_c$ controls both the suppression of the off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in colour space and the transition between singlet and octet states. We find, however, that colour decoherence sets in earlier than the full decoherence of the density matrix, thereby marking the onset of classical behaviour in the system. Finally, we investigate the corrections beyond the quasi-factorised picture due to the quantum diffusion term in $p_\perp$ of the Lindblad equation and we find that these corrections are mild.

Abstract: We review the flavor phenomenology of light dark particles, focusing on axion-like particles with sub-GeV masses and generic flavor-violating couplings. Such states can naturally emerge from the spontaneous breaking of generic flavored symmetries, and are motivated by dark matter or the Strong CP Problem, with the QCD axion serving as a paradigmatic example. Light dark particles can be produced in two-body decays of Standard Model particles, giving rise to missing energy signals that can not only be observed in high-precision flavor experiments, but also be probed in core-collapse supernovae and the cosmic microwave background. These decays are controlled by dimension-five operators, which makes dedicated laboratory searches sensitive to very large UV scales up to $10^{12} {\rm GeV}$ and thus highly complementary to astrophysical and cosmological probes. We provide a comprehensive survey of the resulting limits and prospects across all relevant channels, highlighting the central role of flavor physics in exploring the landscape of light dark matter.

While this limit is straightforward for most contributions, it turns out to be rather nontrivial for certain ones. Numerical analyses show that the results obtained from the two approaches are consistent with each other. We further clarify the origin of the large discrepancies reported in the literature between full QCD sum rule and HQET sum rule results. This work contributes to a deeper understanding of the relationship between the two sum-rule approaches and is expected to facilitate future applications of QCD and HQET sum rules in the study of heavy-flavor hadrons. ### [81] [Moduli spaces of type II twists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07737)
*Fabian Hahner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了平坦背景中IIA型超引力的扭超荷，通过研究10维(1,1)超庞加莱代数中平方为零元在Spin群作用下的轨道；结合IIB型最新结果，完成了II型超引力扭模空间的描述；并讨论其十一维起源、纯旋量形式下与世界面扭的对应，以及T对偶性在IIA/IIB扭之间的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统刻画II型超引力的扭结构，统一理解其低能极限与弦论世界面理论的对应，并建立IIA与IIB扭之间的对偶关系。

Method: 运用表示论与李代数方法，分析10维(1,1)超庞加莱代数中Q²=0的超荷轨道；结合纯旋量超弦形式、Kaluza–Klein约化及广义T对偶性进行物理诠释。

Result: 给出了IIA超引力所有可能扭的完备分类；阐明其与十一维超引力约化的关系；建立了扭与世界面混合A/B模型的对应；明确了T对偶如何将IIA扭映射至IIB扭。

Conclusion: IIA与IIB超引力的扭模空间已完全刻画，且二者通过T对偶等价；该分类为构造拓扑超引力、理解AdS/CFT中的扭曲场论及探索M理论紧化提供了代数基础。

Abstract: We provide a complete classification of twisting supercharges for type IIA supergravity in a flat background by determining the orbits of square-zero elements in the (1,1) super Poincaré algebra in ten dimensions under the action of the Spin group. Together with recent results for type IIB, this completes the description of the moduli space of twists for type II supergravities. Further, we discuss the origins of these twists as dimensional reductions from eleven dimensions. Using the pure spinor formulation of the superstring, we relate the target space classification to worldsheet twists and identify supergravity counterparts of mixed A/B models. Finally, we work out the action of T-duality that relates twists of type IIA with those of type IIB.

</details>

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中半BPS Wilson线上的新算符插入，发现这些算符构成位移超多重态，其维度为一的超原初算符定义了缺陷理论的边缘相关形变，并通过OPE系数与β函数关联加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Wilson线作为规范理论基本探针时，在丰富表示下支持的新算符插入结构，特别是其在缺陷共形场论中的形变性质。

Method: 利用半BPS Wilson线的对称性分析算符谱，识别位移超多重态的超原初算符；结合缺陷CFT的重整化群分析，计算β函数并关联到OPE系数；在弱耦合下显式计算四点函数以验证边缘相关性。

Result: 证明该类维度为一的超原初算符诱导边缘相关的缺陷形变；建立其β函数与特定OPE系数之间的精确关系；完成任意规范群和表示下的四点函数弱耦合计算。

Conclusion: Wilson线在足够丰富的表示下承载新型边缘相关形变，其物理本质由位移超多重态的超原初算符刻画，这拓展了缺陷CFT中可容许形变的范畴，并为AdS/CFT中边界条件的动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Wilson lines are fundamental probes of gauge theories. We show that, in sufficiently rich representations, they support a large new class of operator insertions. For half-BPS lines in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM many of these operators have the quantum numbers of the displacement supermultiplet. Their dimension-one superprimaries define natural deformations of the defect theory. By analyzing the associated beta functions, and relating them to specific OPE coefficients, we show that the deformations are marginally relevant. We support our finding with a weak-coupling computation of the four-point function of these operators for any gauge group and representation.

</details>


### [83] [Non-uniqueness of boundary-value problems in Renormalization Group flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07868)
*Astrid Eichhorn,Zois Gyftopoulos,Aaron Held*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究重整化群流中的边值问题，指出当beta函数雅可比矩阵具有复本征值时，边值问题可能不具唯一解，并给出非唯一性的判据及在标准模型与渐近安全量子引力中的应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受物理系统中不同耦合常数在不同能标上受约束的情形启发，作者将重整化群流从通常的初值问题推广至边值问题。

Method: 通过分析重整化群方程组的边界条件结构，研究其解的存在性与唯一性；引入beta函数雅可比矩阵的谱性质（特别是复本征值）作为非唯一性的判据；辅以具体模型验证。

Result: 发现n个耦合的边值问题即使给出n个边界条件，也不一定有唯一解；当beta函数雅可比矩阵存在复本征值时，解可能出现多重性；提出适用于多耦合系统的非唯一性诊断工具。

Conclusion: 重整化群边值问题具有不同于初值问题的数学结构，其解的非唯一性由beta函数的线性化稳定性谱决定，该现象对标准模型能标演化及渐近安全引力的紫外完备性具有潜在影响。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group flow connects microscopic to macroscopic descriptions of a system and is therefore typically considered as an initial-value problem. Motivated by situations in which different couplings within a system of Renormalization Group equations are constrained at different scales, we instead consider boundary-value problems in Renormalization Group flows. We find that, unlike initial-value problems which provide $n$ conditions for $n$ couplings, boundary-value problems which provide $n$ conditions for $n$ couplings do not always have a unique solution. When the Jacobian matrix, i.e., the matrix of first derivatives of beta functions, has complex eigenvalues, boundary-value problems may be non-unique. We provide a diagnostic tool for non-uniqueness in systems with many couplings. We also provide two examples with potential relevance for physics, namely within the Standard Model as well as within the Einstein-Hilbert truncation of asymptotically safe quantum gravity.

</details>


### [84] [Spin-charge deconfinement and emergent $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ structure from a self-consistent dressing of Fierz-complete $(1+1)$d Dirac fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07955)
*L. Haddad,K. Gonzales,J. Kahanek,M. Kolding,J. Maguire,V. Palombi,H. Truelson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于(1+1)d配对狄拉克费米子中自旋-电荷分离的最新推导，构建了Fierz完备四费米子模型的自洽 dressed 场表示 ψ = Uχ，并揭示其手征-双费米子相结构；通过复合联络 A^dress_μ = i(∂_μU)U⁻¹ 刻画狄拉克算符局域平凡化的障碍，统一了自旋-电荷分离、半无限威尔逊线与平联络全息三类非微扰构造；发现三个物相由一个涌现的 sl(2,ℝ) 规范场耦合自旋与电荷自由度，手征-双费米子相变即为其退禁闭相变，由 boost-sector 闭合威尔逊圈的面积律及可计算弦张力表征；最终将序参量流形几何化为 AdS₃ ≅ SL(2,ℝ)，并与 AdS₃/CFT₂ 的运动学阶段精确对应，提出该 dressed 模型实现 AdS₃ σ-模型的逆Pohlmeyer约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一自旋-电荷分离、规范理论中的威尔逊线构造与平联络全息等非微扰现象，并为Faddeev–Niemi猜想（即自旋-电荷分离与禁闭之间的联系）提供严格实现；同时寻求强关联系统中涌现几何与AdS/CFT结构的微观起源。

Method: 采用自洽dressing变换 ψ(x) = U(x)χ(x)，引入复合规范联络 A^dress_μ = i(∂_μU)U⁻¹ 刻画狄拉克算符的拓扑障碍；结合Fierz完备四费米子模型，分析其手征、双费米子及中间相的相结构；利用sl(2,ℝ)代数结构识别涌现规范场；通过boost-sector威尔逊圈的围道行为（周长律→面积律）诊断退禁闭相变，并计算弦张力；将序参量空间提升至AdS₃ ≅ SL(2,ℝ)，建立与AdS₃/CFT₂运动学的对应。

Result: 证明了一个平凡化定理，统一三类非微扰构造；确认手征-双费米子相变为sl(2,ℝ)规范场的退禁闭相变，由boost-sector威尔逊圈的面积律刻画，且弦张力可显式计算；确立序参量流形为AdS₃，其几何结构与AdS₃/CFT₂的kinematic stage精确匹配；提出该dressed模型实现AdS₃非线性σ模型的逆Pohlmeyer约化。

Conclusion: 自旋-电荷分离本质上是sl(2,ℝ)规范结构的退禁闭现象，其非微扰动力学由AdS₃几何编码；该工作不仅实现了Faddeev–Niemi猜想的具体场论实现，也为强关联系统中AdS/CFT对偶提供了可控的低维构造范例。

Abstract: Building on a recent derivation of spin-charge separation in $(1+1)$d paired Dirac fermions~\cite{Haddad2024}, we develop a self-consistent dressing $ψ(x) = U(x)χ(x)$ for the full Fierz-complete four-fermion model, extending that result and providing a more detailed resolution of the chiral-difermion phase structure. A key feature of this approach is that the composite connection $A_μ^{\rm dress} = i(\partial_μU)U^{-1}$ encodes obstructions to local trivialization of the Dirac operator, i.e., the degree to which the background can be absorbed into the dressing. Using this fact, we prove a trivialization theorem under which three nonperturbative constructions are unified: spin-charge separation in correlated fermion systems, half-infinite Wilson-line dressing in gauge theory, and the holonomy of flat connections. Our approach shows that the three regimes of our model (chiral, difermion, intermediate), are then tied together by an emergent $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ gauge field that binds the spin and charge degrees of freedom. In particular, the chiral-difermion transition is a deconfinement transition for these degrees of freedom, diagnosed by closed boost-sector Wilson loops that develop an area law in the chiral phase for which we compute the associated string tension. This provides a concrete realization of the conjectured Faddeev--Niemi link between spin-charge separation and confinement. We close with a unifying geometric picture in which the order-parameter manifold takes hyperbolic form $ρ^2 - |Δ|^2 = σ^2$, promoted to $\mathrm{AdS}_3 \cong \mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ on inclusion of the charge and difermion phases. The structural matching to the kinematic stage of $\mathrm{AdS}_3/\mathrm{CFT}_2$ is identified explicitly, with the conjecture that the dressed model realizes the inverse Pohlmeyer reduction of the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ sigma model.

</details>


### [85] [Weyl conformal geometry vs Riemannian geometry of Weyl invariant dressed metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08080)
*D. M. Ghilencea,V. -M. Mandric*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Weyl共形几何作为Weyl群（包含标度变换与Poincaré对称性）规范理论的自然几何基础，提出通过标度Wilson线构造‘ dressed ’场与非局域度规 $g^*_{\mu\nu}$，将Weyl不变作用量（如Weyl二次引力与WDBI）映射为Riemann几何下的等效形式；在对称相中该映射非局域且UV下算符不可对易，而在Weyl规范场 $\omega_\mu$ 获得质量并破缺对称后，恢复可交换性与爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建无Weyl反常的量子引力候选理论，需厘清Weyl规范对称性的几何实现及其在对称破缺前后与标准Riemann几何的对应关系。

Method: 采用Weyl规范场论框架，引入标度变换的Wilson线对场与度规进行‘dressing’，定义非局域度规 $g^*_{\mu\nu}$，将Weyl几何中的规范不变作用量重写为基于 $g^*_{\mu\nu}$ 的Riemann形式，并分析 $\omega_\mu$ 运动方程与dressing操作的对易性。

Result: 证明Weyl二次引力与WDBI作用量在Weyl对称相中等价于基于非局域度规 $g^*_{\mu\nu}$ 的Riemann作用量，但 $\omega_\mu$ 的运动方程不与dressing对易；当 $\omega_\mu$ 获得质量并脱耦（对称破缺），dressing可交换，恢复局部Riemann结构与爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量。

Conclusion: Weyl共形几何可通过dressed Riemann描述实现量子一致的规范化，其对称破缺机制自然导出广义相对论低能有效理论，为无反常量子引力提供几何统一框架。

Abstract: Weyl conformal geometry is the natural underlying geometry of gauge theories of the Weyl group (of dilatations and Poincaré symmetry), such as Weyl quadratic gravity and its generalisation, Weyl-Dirac-Born-Infeld action (WDBI). These gauge theories are Weyl-anomaly-free candidates for quantum gravity. We describe Weyl gauge symmetry from a more familiar Riemannian view of Weyl gauge invariant dressed fields by the Wilson line of dilatations. Weyl geometry can then be seen as Riemannian geometry of non-local dressed metric ($g_{μν}^*$), at the cost of non-commutativity in the UV, also induced by the Wilson line. Then Weyl quadratic gravity and WDBI actions of Weyl geometry, which are Weyl gauge invariant in $d$ dimensions, have the same expression in Riemannian geometry defined by $g^*_{μν}$. This is a non-local map between the two geometries and actions in the symmetric phase. Unlike for the metric, the equation of motion of the Weyl gauge field ($ω_μ$) does not commute with the dressing of the metric. When $ω_μ$ becomes massive and decouples (broken phase), commutativity and Einstein-Hilbert action are recovered.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于BRST量子化框架构造作用量；解析分析费米子质量矩阵的非厄米结构及其传播子极点分布；显式计算φ†φ两点函数的一圈费米子图，利用复共轭极点配对性质证明虚部相消；借助BRST代数论证动力学的幂次可重整性。

Result: 一阶微扰下φ†φ两点函数的费米子一圈修正，在去除路径积分权重中的i因子后，对实外动量给出实数值；传播子的复共轭极点结构与BRST对称性共同确保该实性及紫外行为的可控性。

Conclusion: 非厄米质量矩阵不必然破坏物理观测量的实性；BRST对称性可作为非厄米场论中维持可重整性与准幺正性的统一组织原理。

Abstract: A field theory in $3+1$ dimensions contains two fermions, two Abelian gauge fields, and one complex scalar, with dynamics fixed by a BRST symmetry. For a specific parameter configuration, the fermion mass matrix becomes non-Hermitian, and the propagators exhibit complex conjugate poles. We evaluate the one-loop fermion contribution to the two-point function of the composite operator $φ^{\dagger}φ$. Once the factor $i$ of the $e^{iS}$ normalization is removed, the contribution is real for real external momentum, which follows from the pairing of complex conjugate terms in the loop integral. The BRST construction of the action sets up a proof of renormalizability.

</details>

Abstract: We study conserved worldsheet charges associated with spacetime supersymmetry in heterotic pure-spinor superstrings on curved ten-dimensional superspace backgrounds. Requiring BRST invariance and worldsheet conservation gives a covariant set of superspace constraints. In flat superspace these conditions reproduce the standard ten-dimensional supersymmetry generator. In curved superspace, they organize the requirements for global supersymmetry in terms of a normalizable spinor superfield.

Abstract: We investigate the collision dynamics of complex kink solutions in the complex sine-Gordon (CSG) model, focusing on the influence of the relative phase and initial velocity. The model's internal \( U(1) \) symmetry gives rise to a variety of solitary wave solutions, including complex kinks, radiative profiles, and Q-ball configurations. Through numerical simulations, we reveal rich and nontrivial phase-dependent behaviors such as the emergence of red and blue critical speeds, radiative emissions, bion and breather formations, and phase-sensitive oscillation modes. Moreover, we identify extreme values in energy and field quantities at the collision point, uncovering discontinuities that signify transition thresholds in the dynamical system. These findings underscore the complex interplay between internal degrees of freedom and dynamical variables in non-integrable soliton systems, offering new insights into field theories with internal symmetries.

### [89] [Stochastic Survival near Swampland Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08244)
*Omer Guleryuz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Swampland约束与紧致化数据结合随机宇宙学，构建模场在宇宙时间尺度上的生存概率框架；通过Doob变换导出受控区域内的历史演化漂移项，并揭示其在硬边界附近的普适内向壁响应特性，从而为塔/物种截断、弱耦合极限等量子引力控制判据提供随机边界层解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland纲领与紧致化数据指出了有效场论（EFT）失效的临界区域，而随机宇宙学需刻画在此类控制边缘仍能存续的宇宙历史；亟需一个能融合量子引力约束与随机动力学的定量框架。

Method: 将模场演化建模为受控区域内的随机过程，引入硬损失面（hard loss surfaces）、软退化剖面（soft degradation profiles）、有限视界及随机生成元，定义生存概率及其对数——生存作用量；应用Doob变换，将生存代价转化为条件漂移项；分析正则硬边界下法向扩散非零时的普适行为。

Result: 在正则硬边界且法向扩散非零条件下，幸存历史展现出普适的内向壁响应，仅依赖于固有距离与法向扩散系数；塔/物种截断、弦/KK能标、势能判据等均自然获得随机边界层结构，而非微观力；逆映射可判定给定条件漂移是否相容于某标量操作损失面，并重构其法向Doob类。

Abstract: It is a long-standing problem to prove that the number of distinct topological types of Calabi-Yau threefolds is finite. Abstract: We present classical solutions for magnetic monopoles and dyons induced by global monopoles for string-inspired models, in the presence of gravity. Two distinct scenarios are analyzed. In the first, magnetic monopoles arise from the coupling of a non-trivial massless dilaton field to the electromagnetic (EM) sector. In the second, dyonic solutions emerge in the presence of a non-trivial Kalb-Ramond (KR) axion field and a massless dilaton field, which couples the KR and EM sectors. In both models, the monopole and dyon configurations originate from a global monopole associated with the spontaneous breaking of a global O(3) symmetry. The EM sector is described by Born-Infeld electrodynamics, ensuring regularity of the fields at the core of the monopole and a finite self-energy. The resulting solutions represent particle-like configurations with a well-defined core and positive ADM (Arnowitt-Deser-Misner) mass, and are shown to satisfy all standard energy conditions. We also demonstrate the existence of a minimum nonzero magnetic charge in the limiting case of a magnetic monopole with vanishing mass. We also discuss stability criteria for our magnetic-monopole and dyon solutions. We first demonstrate that the mechanical-stability criteria for these solutions are satisified. These require the finiteness of the total force components and also the outward-pointing nature of the radial component, which indicates the avoidance of collapse. Next we analyse the dynamical (linear perturbative) stability of the self-gravitating Born-Infeld monopole and dyon solutions within Einstein-Born--Infeld-dilaton-axion theory in the Gervalle-Volkov framework, and demonstrate that both monopole and dyonic configurations are linearly stable against electromagnetic perturbations in the exterior region, with dyons exhibiting a birefringent helicity structure due to axion-induced mixing.

Abstract: For over four decades, Gribov's no-pole condition has been almost exclusively explored in Euclidean space, where the elliptic nature of the Faddeev-Popov operator provides a clear spectral boundary. In physical Minkowski spacetime, however, this operator becomes a hyperbolic wave operator, and the Euclidean positivity criterion collapses. We show that the natural Lorentzian replacement is a real-time boundary-value problem: a gauge configuration remains inside the first Gribov region as long as the Faddeev-Popov wave equation admits no source-free solutions obeying the Feynman boundary condition. For backgrounds localized in time, this condition translates to the injectivity of the negative-frequency block of the classical ghost scattering map. For stationary backgrounds, it becomes a spatial bound-state or threshold-resonance problem under Fourier transformation. Using a Wronskian identity, we prove that pure frequency mixing in stable self-adjoint time-dependent channels is structurally protected and cannot by itself produce the obstruction. While static chromomagnetic backgrounds reproduce the familiar zero-frequency horizon crossing, static chromoelectric potentials reach the horizon at finite, non-vanishing frequency - a uniquely Lorentzian phenomenon arising because $A_0$ couples directly to the ghost time derivative. We also cast the condition in Fredholm form and show that the exact restriction is a functional determinant, which the naive local continuation of the Gribov-Zwanziger action fails to reproduce, leaving the construction of a genuine local real-time action as the central open problem.

Abstract: We study the post-inflationary open-string Hagedorn phase in perturbatively moduli stabilized brane-antibrane inflation. In this class of models, the volume modulus is stabilized by perturbative corrections rather than by non-perturbative effects to the superpotential, thereby avoiding the standard brane-antibrane $η$-problem. Since inflation ends through tachyon condensation and brane-antibrane annihilation, the endpoint is intrinsically stringy and need not be described immediately by an ordinary radiation bath. We analyze whether the energy released at annihilation can drive the visible sector into an open-string Hagedorn phase, and study the consequences for dark radiation. If the Standard Model (SM) branes lie in the same throat as where the annihilation occurs, we find that a modest fraction of the released energy deposited into surviving visible open strings is sufficient to enter the Hagedorn regime and can suppress the effective number of relativistic species $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ below current observational bounds. If the SM lies in a different throat, the result depends on inter-throat energy transfer: prompt or delayed transfer can still yield a visible Hagedorn phase. However, the mechanism is most efficient when the local string scale in the SM throat is lower than or comparable to that in the annihilation throat.

Abstract: In this paper we clarify the relation between Geometric Thermodynamics and Information Geometry based on the Fisher matrix. On the macroscopic odd-dimensional contact manifold of thermodynamic variables, we introduce for the first time a metric, whose pull-back on the isoentropic symplectic submanifolds transverse to the Reeb field is Kählerian. The pull-back of such metric on equilibrium states, that are lagrangian submanifolds, is the Fisher Hessian. Then we consider the Souriau-like Thermodynamics that uses Calabi-Vesentini (CV) manifolds as Kaehlerian microscopic event manifolds and the Killing moment maps as observable functions. A systematic use of the theory of compact abelian structures and the setup of Special Kähler Geometry in which CV manifolds are encoded allows us to perform the explicit integration defining the partition function for any entry in the CV Tits Satake universality class. The additional actions completing the abelian structure are non linear Casimir functions of the Killing moment-maps and suggest a generalization of Souriau thermodynamics that partially breaks the isometry group symmetry by means of the non vanishing mean values of the Casimir functions in a manner similar to the spontaneous magnetization in ferromagnetism. Our new exact Gibbs distributions provide the analogue for Cartan Neural Networks of the Gaussian probability distributions in flat space used in conventional Machine Learning.

</details>

Conclusion: 该解为NS-NS扇区提供了首个完备解析旋转真空基准，揭示了H-通量与旋转耦合导致的新型几何记忆与轴向非黎曼退化，为Double Field Theory中超越黎曼几何的物理实现提供了具体候选背景。

Abstract: We construct an exact rotating vacuum of the NS--NS sector, equivalently a Double Field Theory vacuum. The construction applies compact $\mathbf{SO}(2)$ S-duality to a rotating Einstein--scalar seed. The solution has four independent parameters $\{\mathfrak{m},j,\mathfrak{q},ζ\}$. To our knowledge, this is the first explicit rotating solution of the pure NS--NS vacuum equations with all three NS--NS fields $\{g,B,φ\}$ determined analytically, independent dilaton and $H$-flux charges, and no Maxwell sector. The static limit is obtained by taking the rotation parameter $j\to 0$. In this limit the geometry is not the spherical Burgess--Myers--Quevedo solution. Instead, it is an axial Zipoy--Voorhees branch carrying $H$-flux, so an oblate deformation remains after rotation is switched off. This geometric memory is absent in pure general relativity and in Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton--axion. The two static branches nevertheless share the same $\ell=0$ parametrized post-Newtonian data $\{MG,β_{\mathrm{PPN}},γ_{\mathrm{PPN}},h\}$. They give two inequivalent NS--NS geometries at identical monopole charges, with the degeneracy lifted at $\ell=2$. At the Kerr horizon locus the outer shell is generically singular in curvature. Above the threshold $|\mathfrak{q}|>\sqrt{\mathfrak{m}^{2}-j^{2}}$, polar geodesics are repelled outward and the rotation axis becomes regular in curvature at the shell. On that axis the inverse metric $g^{μν}$ stays finite while the lower-index Riemannian metric components diverge, whereas off the axis the inverse metric itself diverges. This axis-local degeneracy may offer a setting for non-Riemannian geometry in Double Field Theory, where $g_{μν}$ is not fundamental and the $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$ variables $\{d,\mathcal{H}_{AB}\}$ remain well defined.

</details>

Method: 基于含后牛顿修正的量子引力耦合模型，将玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体置于谐振子势阱中作为非高斯性探测器；利用Feshbach共振调控原子间电磁相互作用至零，确保观测到的非高斯性纯源于量子引力。

Conclusion: BEC体系在实验上具备甄别量子引力非高斯信号的能力；该方案为基于量子信息的量子引力现象学提供了可实施的路径。

Abstract: In a recent work \href{https://doi.org/10.1103/PRXQuantum.2.010325}{PRX QUANTUM 2 (2021) 010325}, a new way of investigating quantum gravity signatures using quantum information theoretic techniques, have been proposed. The primary result of this analysis revealed that non-Gaussianity can arise only through the consideration of a quantum model for the gravity part. Compared to classical gravity, only quantum gravity can result in non-quadratic operators in the Hamiltonian which leads to the non-Gaussian behavior. In our current analysis, we have considered a more realistic scenario taking into effect leading order post-Newtonian corrections in the analysis. We have stayed with the same model of a Bose-Einstein condensate placed inside a harmonic trap potential which indeed works as the detector of the non-Gaussianity generated due to quantum gravitational effects. Bose-Einstein condensates are experimentally well studied; apart from being a single quantum system, they include Feshbach resonances, which helps tuning the strength of the electromagnetic interactions which in principle can be set to zero. This is important since it can help distinguish quantum gravity from electromagnetic interactions without affecting gravitational interactions, and any non-Gaussianity can then be solely attributed to quantum gravity. We observe that the signal to noise ratio gets slightly damped due to the post-Newtonian effects taken under consideration.

</details>

Abstract: We propose and study all-multiplicity building blocks for tree-level string amplitudes in AdS. Abstract: We formulate a semiclassical stress tensor deformation dictionary in the context of the dS/CFT correspondence. Using the metric-flow formulation, we propose that stress tensor deformations of the putative boundary theory are encoded holographically as mixed boundary conditions for the bulk metric at future infinity. The coupled flow equations determine the deformed boundary metric and stress tensor, thereby specifying the source--response relation of the deformed boundary theory. We test the proposal in Kerr-dS$_3$/CFT$_2$, where the conserved charges constructed from the holographic boundary stress tensor agree exactly with the boundary spectrum obtained from the field-theoretic flow equation, providing a nontrivial consistency check of the dictionary. As an application, we compute the holographic pseudo entropy of boundary intervals from complexified geodesic saddles in the deformed Kerr-dS$_3$ geometry, and present explicit results for the $T\bar{T}$ and root-$T\bar{T}$ deformations.

### [102] [Asymptotic Algebras and Holography of Information in CGHS Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09346)

Abstract: Holography of Information (HoI) and the island formula are two recent approaches to the black hole information loss paradox that yield different Page curves. The difference between the Page curves has been argued to be rooted in the algebra that each approach focuses on. In this paper, we study the candidate algebras for the CGHS model. Making the usual assumptions that are made in HoI literature, we establish HoI for the CGHS model coupled to a massless scalar by constructing the radiative phase space and asymptotic algebras at future null infinity. We prove that the quantum state of the right-moving modes can be recovered from an arbitrarily small neighbourhood of the right future null infinity. We then argue that if the Hilbert space factorization between left- and right-moving modes holds in the quantum theory of CGHS, then the island entropy cannot be identified with the von Neumann entropy of any one-sided asymptotic algebra, and that this mismatch constitutes a version of the factorization problem for CGHS/RST models. We discuss the possible distinction between the HoI and island approaches for the CGHS model.

</details>

Abstract: Extensive list of 4+4-dimensional Manin triples that was presented recently can be used to find new solutions of supergravity equations via Poisson-Lie T-plurality. For application of the Poisson-Lie T-plurality we identify Manin triples that form various decompositions of the same Drinfeld double. Beside flat backgrounds and plane-parallel waves solving supergravity equations, plurality transformation also produces curved backgrounds with torsion satisfying (generalized) supergravity equations. Many of the Poisson-Lie transformations can be understood as homogeneous Yang-Baxter deformations. Of special interest are the non-unimodular deformations leading to solutions of generalized supergravity equations.

Motivation: 探究非对易几何如何影响AdS黑洞热力学临界行为，特别是Widom线定位与超临界 crossover 的普适性是否受量子引力效应（以非对易参数α表征）破坏。

Method: 采用α-微扰展开计算热力学量与缩放方差Ω；以Ω极值定义Widom线；在临界点附近构造Landau自由能展开，导出两支对称超临界 crossover 曲线L±；分别在正则系综（固定电荷Q）与扩展系综（含压强P为热力学变量）中提取标度指数；辅以数值求解验证相图。

Result: Widom线由Ω的极值唯一确定；超临界 crossover 满足δT ∝ |ΔQ|^{3/2}, δS ∝ |ΔQ|^{1/2}（正则系综）及δP ∝ |ΔT|^{3/2}, δρ ∝ |ΔT|^{1/2}（扩展系综）；标度指数β=1/2、γ=1，严格属于平均场普适类；非对易参数α仅改变标度律前因子（次主导项），不改变普适类。

Conclusion: 非对易修正不改变AdS黑洞超临界相变的平均场普适性，Widom线与超临界 crossover 结构具有鲁棒性，表明该类量子引力修正不破坏经典临界标度行为。

Abstract: We analytically study the Widom line and supercritical crossover of noncommutative charged AdS black holes. Treating the noncommutative parameter $α$ perturbatively, we compute thermodynamic quantities and the scaled variance $Ω$ in both canonical and extended ensembles. The Widom line is identified as the extremum of $Ω$. Using a Landau expansion near the critical point, we derive the two symmetric crossover branches $L^{\pm}$, which obey $δT\sim \left|ΔQ\right|^{β+γ}$, $δS\sim \left|ΔQ \right|^β$ in the canonical ensemble and $δP\sim \left|ΔT\right|^{β+γ}$, $δρ\sim \left|ΔT\right|^β$ in the extended ensemble. These scaling relations conform to the mean-field universality class ($β=1/2$, $γ=1$), and the noncommutative parameter only shifts subleading amplitudes without altering the universality class. Numerical verification and complete supercritical phase diagrams are also presented using supercritical crossover lines. Our results show that noncommutative corrections preserve the mean-field universality of black hole supercriticality.

</details>


### [105] [Effective scalaron--photon interaction in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09510)
*Yuri Shtanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了f(R)引力中scalaron与规范场的有效耦合，特别关注scalaron衰变为双光子的过程；通过Fujikawa方法计算了由迹反常诱导的anomaly-induced贡献，并指出仅考虑经典能量-动量张量迹会得到错误结果；当scalaron质量远小于圈图粒子质量时，图解贡献与反常项精确相消，导致有效耦合和衰变率被强压制；该结果澄清了文献中关于scalaron–光子耦合的长期分歧，根源在于不同量子化方案对迹反常处理的不等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文献中关于f(R)引力中scalaron与无质量规范场（尤其是光子）有效耦合存在的长期分歧，厘清量子效应（特别是能量-动量张量的迹反常）在Jordan与Einstein帧变换中的作用。

Method: 采用Fujikawa方法，在Jordan帧将scalaron视为度规的内禀组分，系统分析其通过能量-动量张量与物质场的耦合所诱发的量子反常；分别在QED和完整标准模型框架下推导scalaron–光子有效相互作用项，并对比仅含经典迹与包含量子迹反常的两种方案。

Result: 得到由迹反常诱导的scalaron–光子耦合项，该结果与包含迹反常的直接微扰计算一致，而不同于仅用经典能量-动量张量迹所得结果；在m_φ ≪ m_f极限下，圈图振幅与反常项严格抵消，导致有效γγ耦合消失，Γ(φ→γγ)被指数级压制。

Conclusion: scalaron–光子耦合的正确量子处理必须包含迹反常的帧变换效应；先前文献中的分歧源于对量子环效应（尤其是迹反常）的不同实现方式；这一结论对scalaron作为暗物质候选者的唯象学（如伽马射线信号、寿命约束）具有决定性影响。

Abstract: We revisit the effective coupling of the scalaron to gauge fields in $f(R)$ gravity minimally coupled to the Standard Model, focusing on the scalaron decay into two photons. Treating the scalaron as an intrinsic component of the Jordan-frame metric, we analyse quantum effects arising from its coupling to the energy--momentum tensor. In this framework, the trace anomaly contributes to the scalaron--gauge boson interaction.
  Using Fujikawa's method, we derive the anomaly-induced contribution, associated with the transformation of matter fields between the Jordan and Einstein frames, first in QED and then in the full Standard Model. The resulting effective scalaron--photon interaction agrees with direct perturbative calculations that include the trace anomaly and differs from the result obtained when only the classical trace of the energy--momentum tensor is taken into account. In the limit where the scalaron mass is much smaller than the masses of particles circulating in the loop, the diagrammatic contribution cancels the anomaly-induced term, leading to a vanishing effective coupling and a strong suppression of the scalaron decay rate into photons.
  These results clarify the origin of discrepancies in the literature concerning the effective scalaron coupling to massless gauge fields. The discrepancies arise from inequivalent prescriptions for implementing quantum loop effects in $f(R)$ gravity, leading to different scalaron--gauge field interactions with direct implications for scalaron dark-matter phenomenology.

</details>


### [106] [Negative heat capacities in spherically symmetric sectors of $d$-matrix quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09521)
*Denjoe O'Connor,Sanjaye Ramgoolam*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有U(N)规范对称性的d-矩阵谐振子中SO(d)与O(d)不变子空间的微观简并度，通过群表示论（Schur-Weyl对偶、Kronecker系数）、调和分析及群积分方法导出简并度Z(N,d,k)的配对公式，并发现其在大N极限下呈现负热容→正热容的'caloric fold'现象，类比AdS黑洞热力学，提出该模型可作为全息黑洞热力学的可解矩阵对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可解的多矩阵量子力学模型，以捕捉AdS黑洞热力学中关键特征（如caloric fold），为全息原理提供具体实现。

Method: 利用Schur-Weyl对偶将不变算符计数转化为S_k群表示的Kronecker系数；结合U(d)/SO(d)上调和分析与U(N)、SO(d)群积分，推导微观简并度Z(N,d,k)的配对表达式；采用矩阵模型与半经典本征值密度分析处理大N,d极限。

Result: 获得Z(N,d,k)的精确配对公式；发现k ≤ N时热容由负变正，存在临界点k_crit ∼ N²/4；大N,d极限下简并度由ribbon graph组合结构主导；确认caloric fold是该模型与AdS黑洞共有的普适热力学特征。

Conclusion: SO(d)/O(d)-不变d-矩阵谐振子是描述全息黑洞热力学（尤其caloric fold）的一个可控、可计算的矩阵模型，其简并结构深刻联系于对称群表示、调和分析与拓扑图计数。

Abstract: We consider the $SO(d)$ and $O(d)$ invariant sectors of the bosonic $d$-matrix harmonic oscillator with $U(N)$ gauge symmetry. The micro-canonical degeneracy $\mathcal{Z}( N , d , k )$ for fixed energy $k$ is expressed as a pairing between an $N$-dependent vector and a $d$-dependent vector in the space of partitions of the integer $k$. This pairing formula is derived by counting invariant words in multi-matrix variables $X^i_{j,a}$, using properties of Clebsch-Gordan multiplicities (Kronecker coefficients) for the symmetric group $S_k$, Schur-Weyl duality and harmonic analysis on the homogeneous space $U(d)/SO(d)$. Analytic formulae for large $N$ and $k$ with $ k \le N $ are obtained using group integrals over $U(N)$ and $SO(d)$ (or $ O(d)$). The micro-canonical heat capacity in this regime is negative and turns positive, at a critical value $k_{\rm crit}$, due to finite $N$ modifications to the counting, thus forming what we denote as a characteristic caloric fold in the $ E $ versus $T$ curve. Data from the pairing formula is well fitted by $k_{\rm crit} \sim { N^2 \over 4 }$ for small values of $d$. A derivation of this large $N$ formula is given using a matrix model approximation and semi-classical analysis of the eigenvalue density. The large $N,d$ limit of the degeneracies reveals a key role for ribbon graph combinatorics. The caloric fold is also notably a property of black hole thermodynamics in anti-de-Sitter spaces. We propose the spherically symmetric \(SO(d)\) and \(O(d)\) invariant sectors of \(d\)-matrix quantum mechanics as tractable matrix systems for capturing key features of dual descriptions of black-hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [107] [Double-Current Deformations of Two-Dimensional QFTs with Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09752)
*Zhengyuan Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了具有背景规范场反常的二维量子场论的双流形变形，通过引入动力学规范场、紧致Stueckelberg场及反常线丛上的平行输运，保持变形前后反常不变；在平直背景规范场下，非零模Stueckelberg场将动力学规范场局域化为平坦联络，使变形退化为有限维全纯性积分；文中给出了环面及高亏格情形下的积分核，并应用于紧致玻色子（即U(1) WZW模型），得到其配分函数的高斯变换，谱由k→K_λ映射，接触项与谱流数据由原始反常控制；最后推广至反常相容的非阿贝尔与齐次Yang–Baxter形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有背景规范场反常的二维QFT在双流形变形下如何保持反常结构的问题，统一处理Abelian与non-Abelian情形，并建立与Stueckelberg机制、anomaly line bundle和holonomy integral的几何路径积分框架。

Method: 基于路径积分方法，将种子理论耦合至动力学规范场与紧致Stueckelberg标量场，在anomaly line bundle上插入平行输运算子；利用Stueckelberg非零模对平坦背景下的动力学规范场进行局域化约束，导出环面及高亏格上的积分核；对U(1) WZW模型显式计算高斯型变换，并推广至non-Abelian与homogeneous Yang-Baxter形变。

Result: 证明双流形变形后配分函数保留原反常；获得环面与高亏格上精确的积分核表达式；对紧致玻色子给出谱映射k→K_λ及受原反常控制的接触项与谱流；建立anomaly-compatible non-Abelian和homogeneous YB形变框架。

Conclusion: 双流形变形可系统地延拓至具规范反常的二维QFT，其几何实现依赖Stueckelberg机制与anomaly line bundle的平行输运；该构造保持反常不变性，且自然涵盖Abelian、non-Abelian与integrable形变，为反常匹配与对偶性研究提供新路径积分工具。

Abstract: We construct the double-current deformations of two-dimensional quantum field theories whose partition functions have background gauge-field anomalies. Extending the path integral construction of [1], we couple the seed theory to dynamical gauge fields and compact Stueckelberg fields and insert parallel transport in the anomaly line bundle. The deformed partition function then has the same anomaly as the undeformed one.
  For flat background gauge fields the Stueckelberg non-zero modes localize the dynamical gauge fields to flat connections, reducing the deformation to a finite-dimensional holonomy integral. We derive the integral kernel on the torus and its higher-genus generalization. For the compact boson, or equivalently the Abelian $U(1)$ WZW model, the kernel gives a Gaussian transform of the torus partition function: at zero background the spectrum is obtained by $k\to K_λ$, while contact terms and spectral-flow data remain controlled by the original anomaly. We also formulate the anomaly-compatible non-Abelian and homogeneous Yang-Baxter generalization.

</details>


### [108] [Topological defects and scalar field modes in warped geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09781)
*A. A. Saharian,E. L. Karapetyan,G. V. Mirzoyan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个通用框架，研究拓扑缺陷对弯曲时空背景下量子标量场局域性质的影响，通过分离变量法求解场方程，得到归一化模函数，并应用于AdS中全局单极子的Hadamard双点函数计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑缺陷（如宇宙弦、全局单极子）在弯曲时空（尤其是具有翘曲几何和角缺陷的背景）中对量子场论效应（如真空极化、Casimir效应、粒子产生）的影响，特别是其局域可观测量（如能量动量张量、两点函数）的修正。

Method: 采用微分几何方法分解Ricci张量与曲率标量；对任意曲率耦合参数ξ，将标量场方程在翘曲几何+角缺陷背景中实现完全变量分离（径向、角向、翘曲坐标）；构造并归一化完整正交模函数系；应用谱表示法计算Hadamard有限部分的两点函数。

Result: 获得了适用于任意角亏缺参数的完备归一化模函数；导出了翘曲AdS时空中介质含全局单极子时的Hadamard双点函数显式表达式；揭示了角缺陷与负曲率协同导致的非平直真空结构特征。

Conclusion: 拓扑缺陷的角几何结构与翘曲几何的耦合显著改变量子标量场的模结构与真空期望值，该框架可系统推广至更高自旋场及更广义的缺陷构型（如非阿贝尔缺陷、多缺陷叠加）。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for investigating the influence of topological defects on the local characteristics of a quantum scalar field in a warped geometry background. The Ricci tensor and curvature scalar are decomposed into contributions from the warp factor, the radial geometry and the angular defect structure. For an arbitrary curvature coupling parameter, the field equation is separated into independent radial, angular and warp-coordinate parts. A complete set of normalized mode functions is obtained for general values of the angular deficit parameters. The general formalism is applied to several specific cases, such as conformally flat warped spacetimes, generalized cosmic strings, global monopoles and anti-de Sitter (AdS)-type warped geometries. The Hadamard two-point function is then evaluated for a global monopole in AdS spacetime using the obtained mode functions.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [109] [The classical boundaries of the EPR argument and quantum ontology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07826)
*Vincenzo Chilla*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出希尔伯特空间经典力学（HCM），主张经典性源于逻辑上的布尔性（即态的互易对易性），而非传统ℏ→0的动力学极限；通过EPR准则、定域性与测量独立性，标准量子力学可约化为HCM；进而提出一种基于观测环境—被观对象二分结构的量子本体论，并区分ontic、processional与tropos-existential三类存在模式，重构客观实在判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子-经典过渡的逻辑基础问题，澄清Bohr-Einstein关于‘客观实在’的长期歧义，回应EPR论证中隐含的经典预设与量子形式体系的内在张力。

Method: 以冯·诺依曼希尔伯特空间公理体系为起点，施加布尔逻辑约束（即所有可准备态两两对易）导出HCM；结合EPR实在性判据、定域性与测量独立性进行理论约化；引入结构性二分（observational environment / observed object）构建分层本体论框架，并定义三重存在范畴。

Result: 1) 经典性本质是逻辑布尔性而非动力学极限；2) 标准量子力学在EPR+定域+测量独立下退化为HCM；3) EPR不揭示量子不完备，而暴露其前提中的隐含经典边界；4) 提出‘描述性客观性仅为物理实在的充分非必要条件’的新实在判据。

Conclusion: 量子形式体系自身已内蕴经典性逻辑条件；真正的量子本体论需放弃‘全域客观描述’预设，转向语境依赖的结构性二分模型，从而统一可观测现象与干涉等非客观现象。

Abstract: Von Neumann's Hilbert-space formalism of quantum mechanics constitutes a logico-physical theory of observed or measured reality. Imposing the logical constraint of Booleanity, essential for objectively shareable descriptions among observers, reveals the physical meaning of classicality inherently embedded within the formalism itself. Starting from this consideration, the present work reformulates the quantum-classical transition via Hilbert-space classical mechanics (HCM), grounding classicality not in the dynamical limit ($\hbar \to 0$), but in the logical constraint of Booleanity (i.e., the mutual commutativity of preparable states). Within this state-centric framework, applying the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) criterion alongside locality and measurement independence reduces standard quantum mechanics to the HCM model. Thus, the EPR argument reveals not quantum incompleteness, but the implicit classical boundaries of its own premises. To resolve this impasse, we articulate a nuanced quantum ontology grounded in a fundamental structural bipartition between the observational environment and the observed object, which accommodates three categorical distinctions: ontic, processional, and tropos-existential. Building on this, we propose a criterion of objective reality wherein descriptive objectivity is treated as merely a sufficient condition for physical reality. This addresses the historical Bohr-Einstein ambiguity, enabling the quantum formalism to ontologically unify objective measured phenomena and non-objective observed interference within a context-dependent framework.

</details>


### [110] [Mathematical crystal chemistry: A formal theory for crystal structure prediction by generalized disjunctive programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07927)
*Ryotaro Koshoji*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义析取规划（GDP）的晶体结构预测理论，通过耦合连续变量与布尔变量，并结合图论与在线学习，实现仅依赖原子半径和配位数的高效晶体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体结构预测面临高维连续优化与组合复杂性双重挑战，需建立兼顾化学意义与计算效率的新理论框架。

Method: 构建含连续变量（原子位置、晶胞参数）与布尔变量（键连性、空间群对称性、配位模式）的广义析取规划模型；将晶体结构映射为具有明确化学语义的图（'组合骨架'）；在结构优化过程中动态识别高频子图并进行可行性在线学习；采用交替迭代优化策略处理变量强耦合性。

Result: 实现了仅输入原子半径与可行配位数即可快速预测/设计多种无机晶体结构；显著提升收敛效率，避免陷入局部极小；子图在线学习机制加速最优结构发现。

Conclusion: GDP框架成功将无机结构化学原理形式化为可计算模型，揭示了晶体结构中离散拓扑与连续几何的协同优化本质，为数据稀疏条件下的理性材料设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inorganic structural chemistry leads naturally to a theory for crystal structure prediction formalized by a generalized disjunctive programming (GDP), which is formulated using continuous and Boolean variables to involve the algebraic equations, disjunctions, and logic propositions. Since the feasibilities of continuous variables change drastically depending on Boolean variables and vice versa, iterative optimization of continuous and Boolean variables efficiently transforms a randomly generated initial structure into an optimal solution. Boolean variables are introduced to elucidate the ``combinatorial backbone'' of the original continuous optimization problem, which corresponds to the graphs describing crystal structures with clear chemical meanings. Since many subgraphs are discovered in numerous graphs generated through structural optimizations, on-the-fly learning of the feasibilities of them accelerates the discovery of the optimal solutions. This theory enables designing a wide variety of crystal structures with small computations based on only the atomic radii and feasible coordination numbers of each atom.

</details>


### [111] [Structure of Clifford groups of composite finite quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08215)
*Miroslav Korbelář,Jiří Tolar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了配置空间为 Z_{n1} ⊕ ... ⊕ Z_{nk} 的多体量子系统的Clifford群结构，证明当希尔伯特空间维数 N 为偶数时，Clifford群及其射影版本是自然半直积当且仅当 N 不被 4 整除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体量子系统中Clifford群的代数结构对量子纠错、量子计算和有限维相空间量子力学至关重要；此前关于偶维数情形的结构结果零散且未统一刻画半直积性质。

Method: 利用关联辛群 Sp_{[n1,...,nk]} 的生成元关系，结合群论与有限阿贝尔群上的辛几何，分析Clifford群的分裂性与半直积结构。

Result: 对任意 k ≥ 1 和 n_i ≥ 2，当 N = n_1⋯n_k 为偶数时，Clifford群 C_N 与射影Clifford群 PC_N 均为自然半直积 ⇔ N ≢ 0 mod 4。

Conclusion: Clifford群的半直积结构具有模 4 判据：奇维数恒成立，偶维数仅在 N ≡ 2 mod 4 时成立；该结果统一并推广了既有文献中零散的偶维案例。

Abstract: In this paper the Clifford groups of general multipartite quantum systems with configuration space $\mathbb{Z}_{n_1}\oplus\cdots\oplus\mathbb{Z}_{n_k}$, $k\geq 1$ are studied. It is known that the Clifford group is a natural semidirect product provided the dimension $N=n_1\cdots n_k$ of the corresponding Hilbert space is an odd number.
  For even $N$ special results on the Clifford groups are scattered in the mathematical literature, but they do not concern the semidirect product structure. Using the relation of generators of the associated symplectic group $\mathrm{Sp}_{[n_1,\dots,n_k]}$ we prove that for even $N=n_1\cdots n_k$ both Clifford group and the projective Clifford group are natural semidirect products if and only if $N$ is not divisible by four.

</details>


### [112] [A Unified Framework for Virtual Wave Transform: From Generalized Formulation to Excitation-Specific Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08747)
*Pengfei Zhu,Julien Lecompagnon,Philipp Daniel Hirsch,Mathias Ziegler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一理论框架，通过复频域解析延拓，将扩散过程（抛物型）与虚拟波动场（双曲型）建立谱积分算子映射；该算子是因果、紧致的Fredholm算子，表现为非平稳低通滤波器，揭示扩散过程的信息损失与逆问题的本质不适定性；各类激励方式（脉冲、锁相、扫频、编码）被解释为同一变换在不同子空间上的投影，即其谱结构的差异化采样；框架可推广至矩阵值系统，并提供时空演化在扩散与传播机制间的谱几何诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合扩散型（如热传导）与波动型（如声波/电磁波）动力学之间的理论鸿沟，统一处理虚拟波方法中长期存在的形式化分歧，并为时域激励设计提供严格的算子理论基础。

Method: 构建基于复频率解析延拓的谱积分算子，将实时间扩散场映射至虚波场；证明该映射为因果紧致Fredholm算子；分析其作为非平稳低通滤波器的算子性质；将各类时域激励方案表征为该算子谱分解下的子空间投影。

Result: 1) 扩散与虚拟波之间存在显式、因果、紧致的谱算子映射；2) 扩散过程的信息损失与逆重建的不适定性源于该算子的紧致性与低通特性；3) 所有标准激励方案被统一为同一算子的谱采样策略；4) 框架自然推广至多物理场耦合（矩阵值系统），并引出时空演化的谱几何描述。

Conclusion: 扩散与波动动力学可通过一个统一的谱积分算子框架进行等价描述，该框架不仅解释了现有虚拟波方法的内在一致性，更将激励设计提升为算子谱采样问题，为非傅里叶时域传感与逆问题提供了新的数学物理基础。

Abstract: We present a unified theoretical framework for the mapping between diffusive and wave-like dynamics, formulated as a spectral integral operator acting on temporal fields. By introducing an analytic continuation in the complex frequency plane, we establish an explicit correspondence between thermal diffusion and a virtual wave field governed by a hyperbolic equation. This mapping is shown to define a causal, compact Fredholm operator that acts as a nonstationary low-pass filter, thereby revealing the intrinsic information loss of diffusive processes and the fundamental ill-posedness of the inverse reconstruction. Within this operator framework, we demonstrate that commonly used excitation schemes-including pulse, lock-in, chirped, and coded excitations-emerge as distinct projections onto subspaces of a single underlying transformation, corresponding to different sampling strategies of its spectral structure. This unifies previously disparate virtual wave formulations and provides a systematic interpretation of excitation design in terms of operator sampling and information encoding. The framework further generalizes to matrix-valued systems and suggests a spectral-geometric interpretation of temporal evolution across diffusive and propagative regimes.

</details>


### [113] [Tight-Binding Spectra of Finite Incidence Geometries: From Spatial Localization to $SU(6)$ Flavor Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08753)
*Pawel Nurowski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限几何构型的Levi图上紧束缚量子哈密顿量的谱性质，发现实嵌入导致结构波局域化，复嵌入恢复布洛赫传播；解析得到多个构型的精确谱分解，揭示由几何阻挫驱动的宏观零能平带；并建立这些图与标准模型味对称性的形式同构，将Schläfli图映射到SU(6)味多重态，平带对应超重强子的运动冻结，Cremona-Richmond构型则补全W(E₆)对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离散几何构型如何通过其拓扑与嵌入性质调控量子输运，并寻求高能物理中对称性结构与组合几何之间的深层对应。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型在Levi图上构造哈密顿量；结合实/复射影平面嵌入分析对称性破缺与合成规范相；对具体有限构型（Desargues、Kantor、Schläfli double six、Cremona-Richmond）进行解析谱分解；建立图结构与李群表示（SU(6)、W(E₆)）之间的结构同构映射。

Result: 1) 实平面嵌入破坏平移对称性，引发结构波局域化；2) ℂℙ²嵌入引入合成规范相，恢复布洛赫类传播；3) Schläfli (12₅,30₂) 与 Cremona-Richmond (15₃) 构型存在严格零能宏观平带，源于几何阻挫；4) Schläfli图与SU(6)味多重态存在同构，平带对应超重baryon的动能冻结；5) Cremona-Richmond构型基于三切平面而非点交，代数地完备W(E₆)作用于27条线的对称性。

Conclusion: 有限几何构型的Levi图不仅是抽象组合对象，更是承载非平凡量子动力学与高能对称性结构的物理载体；几何阻挫可作为平带工程的普适机制，而E₆相关构型暗示标准模型味结构可能具有深层射影几何起源。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral properties of tight-binding quantum Hamiltonians defined on the bipartite Levi graphs of finite geometric configurations. In Part I, we analyze the $10_3$ Desargues and Kantor configurations, demonstrating how deterministic spatial deformations induced by real planar embeddings ($\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$) destroy translational symmetry, leading to structural wave localization. We show how embedding in the complex projective plane ($\mathbb{C}\mathbb{P}^2$) restores Bloch wave propagation via synthetic gauge phases. In Part II, we evaluate the Schläfli double six ($12_5, 30_2$) and the Cremona-Richmond ($15_3$) configurations in $\mathbb{R}^3$. We analytically derive their exact spectral decompositions, confirming the existence of macroscopic zero-energy flat bands ($E=0$) driven entirely by geometric frustration. In Part III, we establish a formal structural isomorphism between these discrete tight-binding networks and the flavor symmetry sector of the Standard Model. We map the Schläfli graph to the $SU(6)$ flavor multiplets, where the flat band corresponds to the kinematic freezing of ultra-heavy baryons. Finally, we discuss the complementary Cremona-Richmond $15_3$ topology, demonstrating how its distinct geometric nature (based on tritangent planes rather than point intersections) provides a purely algebraic, topological completion to the $W(E_6)$ symmetry of the 27 lines.

</details>


### [114] [Elastodynamics from a variational standpoint: integral equalities and inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08789)
*Yury Grabovsky,Lev Truskinovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将诺特定理推广至含激波的非线性弹动力学变分极值问题，导出适用于奇异极值曲线的广义守恒律，并揭示在熵解条件下守恒等式退化为不等式；特别地，即使存在激波，动能亦可完全从动态存储弹性能量表达式中消去。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性弹动力学方程的变分极值解（即作用量泛函的极值）可形成激波（shocks），而经典诺特定理仅适用于光滑解，亟需发展适用于此类奇异解的对称性与守恒律理论。

Method: 基于埃米·诺特的经典变分对称性方法，将其适配于具有激波的奇异极值曲线，运用广义变分法推导适用于间断解的积分关系；结合热力学熵条件，分析守恒律在激波处的退化行为。

Result: 1) 获得含激波弹动力学极值解的新积分关系，显式关联动能与弹性能量；2) 对熵解（热力学容许解），诺特等式退化为不等式；3) 动态存储弹性能量表达式中动能项可被完全消去，即使在激波存在下仍成立。

Conclusion: 诺特定理可系统推广至含激波的奇异变分问题，所得广义守恒律不仅保持几何与物理结构，且揭示了惯性能量再分配中动能的可消除性，为非光滑动力学中的对称性分析提供了严格数学框架。

Abstract: As it is well known, solutions of equations of nonlinear elastodynamics, representing extremals of the action functional, can form shocks. We adapt the classical approach of Emmy Noether to such singular extremals and derive the appropriately generalized integral relations within Calculus of Variations. We apply them to elastodynamical extremals with shocks, obtaining new integral relations involving kinetic and elastic energies. For the extremals representing thermodynamically admissible (entropy) solutions of the corresponding hyperbolic Euler-Lagrange equations, the classical equalities, characterizing the variational approach of Noether, expectedly transform into ineqalities. We show that, rather remarkably, despite the crucial role of material velocity in the fully inertial energy redistribution processes, the corresponding kinetic energy can be completely eliminated from the expression for the dynamically stored elastic energy, even in the presence of shocks.

</details>


### [115] [Clapeyron-type theorems in nonlinear elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08818)
*Yury Grabovsky,Lev Truskinovsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了非线性弹性力学中Clapeyron定理的若干新积分关系，将其置于变分法框架下，引入‘部分变分对称性’概念（如尺度不变性、缩放齐次性），并通过Noether分析导出物理力与构型力做功的统一表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典线性弹性中的Clapeyron定理推广至非线性情形，并建立其与变分结构（特别是对称性）的深层联系。

Method: 基于变分原理，识别并定义‘部分变分对称性’；应用Noether定理，结合非线性弹性本构与边界条件，推导广义能量恒等式。

Result: 获得多个非线性Clapeyron型积分恒等式，揭示了物理力与构型力做功的协同结构；证明尺度不变性等部分对称性可自然导出此类恒等式。

Conclusion: Clapeyron定理的本质是特定变分对称性的体现；‘部分变分对称性’为非线性弹性能量恒等式提供了统一而普适的变分基础。

Abstract: Clapeyron's Theorem of classical linear elasticity provides a way to explicitly express the energy stored in an equilibrium configuration in terms of the work of the forces applied on the boundary. We derive several new integral relations which can be viewed as nonlinear analogs of this classical result, reinterpreting them as rather general statements within Calculus of Variations. These relations reflect specific properties of Lagrangians, that we call ``partial variational symmetries'', since they are more general than classical variational symmetries. In the framework of nonlinear elasticity, partial variational symmetries, such as scale invariance, or scaling homogeneity, lead, via Noether's analysis to different nonlinear generalizations of Clapeyron's Theorem that combine naturally the work of physical and configurational forces. We present a series of illuminating examples showing the effectiveness of the obtained general results in different problems of nonlinear elasticity.

</details>


### [116] [Symmetry Regularization of 1D Generalized Coulomb Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08900)
*Zhanqiang Bai,Junwei Ma,Guowu Meng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了两个显式的幺正 intertwining 算子 $\hat\iota_{\pm}$，将一维广义库仑问题中每个能量本征子空间幺正等价于 $\widetilde{\mathrm{SL}}(2,\mathbb{R})$ 的幺正最低权表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一维广义库仑问题（含Ma-Meng-Xiao定义的广义开普勒问题）的量子对称性结构，推广经典正则化映射 $\\iota_{\\pm}$ 到量子层面。

Method: 通过显式构造幺正 intertwining 算子 $\\hat\\iota_{\\pm}$，实现 Hilbert 空间 $L^{2}(\\mathbb{R}_{>0},\\mathrm{d}q)$ 中 $H_κ$-能量本征子空间与 $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 最低权表示之间的幺正同构。

Result: 证明每个能量定值子空间承载一个幺正最低权表示；$\\hat\\iota_{\\pm}$ 是经典正则化映射 $\\iota_{\\pm}$ 的量子对应；揭示了该类系统内在的 $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 对称性。

Conclusion: 一维广义库仑问题具有深层的 $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{SL}}(2,\\mathbb{R})$ 代数结构，其量子对称性可通过显式幺正 intertwining 算子完全刻画。

Abstract: For the 1D generalized Coulomb problems--a family that includes the quantizations of the 1D generalized Kepler problems of Ma-Meng-Xiao \cite{MMX2025}--we construct two explicit unitary intertwiners $\hatι_{\pm}$, the quantum analogs of the classical symmetry regularization maps $ι_{\pm}$ of \cite{MMX2025}, that unitarily identify each $H_κ$-energy-definite portion of the Hilbert space $L^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{>0},\mathrm{d}q)$ with a unitary lowest-weight representation of $\widetilde{\mathrm{SL}}(2,\mathbb{R})$.

</details>


### [117] [On an $n-$Dimensional Travel Time Tomography Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09020)
*Michael V. Klibanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次针对高维（n≥2）旅行时间层析成像问题（TTTP）在形式确定但不完全输入数据下的稳定性估计与唯一性定理展开研究；通过引入半离散化、截断傅里叶类展开及新型Carleman估计，在Carleman加权空间中建立了Lipschitz稳定性估计，从而导出唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自Herglotz（1905）和Wiechert-Zoeppritz（1907）以来百年未解的n维（n≥2）旅行时间层析成像问题在形式确定（p=n）但不完全输入数据下的稳定性与唯一性问题。

Method: 采用半离散化策略：对eikonal方程在n−1个变量上作有限差分离散；将解表示为关于源点位置依赖的特殊正交基的截断傅里叶类级数；引入Carleman加权空间，并构造并应用一个新型Carleman估计。

Result: 在所设半离散与级数展开条件下，证明了Lipschitz型稳定性估计，该估计直接蕴含了解的唯一性。

Conclusion: 首次为形式确定的n维TTTP建立了严格的稳定性与唯一性理论基础；Carleman估计与Carleman加权空间为此类非线性反问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: In their seminal works Herglotz (1905) and Wiechert and Zoeppritz (1907) have solved the so-called Travel Time Tomography Problem (TTTP) in the 1-D case. However, the question about stability estimates and uniqueness theorems for an n-D n>= 2 TTTP with formally determined incomplete input data still mostly stands open after more than one hundred years period. \textquotedblleft Formally determined input data" means that the number p of free variables in the input data equals the number $n$ of free variables in the unknown right hand side of the governing nonliniear eikonal PDE, p=n. Some previous publications demonstrate that it is possible to develop well performed numerical methods for the TTTP with formally determined input data, which indicates the importance of such data for practical applications.
  This is the first publication in which the above question is addressed. More precisely, we consider a semi-discrete case, in which a PDE generated by the eikonal equation is written in finite differences with respect to n-1 variables. In addition, it is assumed that the solution of that semi-discrete PDE is represented via a truncated Fourier-like series with respect to a special orthonormal basis of functions, which depend only on the position of the point source. Under these conditions, Lipschitz stability estimate is proven, and this estimate implies uniqueness. An important tool of this paper is a new Carleman estimate. Carleman Weighted Spaces are introduced. Carleman estimates were not applied previously to address questions about stability estimates and uniqueness theorems for the TTTP.

</details>


### [118] [No-Go Theorem for BEC in the Nelson and Pauli--Fierz Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09538)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过泛函积分表示法构造了Nelson模型、无自旋Pauli-Fierz模型及含自旋Pauli-Fierz模型的KMS态，研究了在移除红外与紫外截断后的点源模型；建立了零模可分辨性、无对角长程序、零模形式消失、凝聚密度为零、序参量判据、BEC方向平凡性及BEC理想平凡性之间的等价性；并统一用预解代数刻画红外商代数与BEC理想，给出空间平移不变KMS态的算子代数充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场论中开放量子系统在热平衡下的代数结构，特别是玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）与红外结构在非相对论QFT模型（如Nelson和Pauli-Fierz）中的严格实现；弥补现有文献中对KMS态构造与BEC判据间代数联系的缺失。

Method: 采用欧几里得泛函积分表示构造KMS态；运用预解代数（resolvent algebra）框架处理红外发散；通过C*-代数与von Neumann代数方法分析零模、BEC理想与红外商；利用空间平移不变性与KMS条件推导等价性判据。

Result: 证明了在测试函数空间能分辨零模的前提下，六种BEC/相变判据彼此等价；给出了三类模型中红外商代数与BEC理想的统一预解代数描述；建立了适用于任意给定平移不变KMS态的算子代数型充分条件。

Conclusion: BEC的物理判据在代数量子统计框架下具有深刻的统一性；预解代数为处理红外问题与热态构造提供了自然且严格的数学语言；该工作为非相对论QFT中热力学极限与自发对称破缺的严格分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct KMS states for the Nelson model, the spinless Pauli--Fierz model, and the Pauli--Fierz model with spin by functional integral representations, and study point-source models after removal of the infrared and ultraviolet cutoffs. If the physical test-function space can distinguish the zero mode, the absence of off-diagonal long-range order, the vanishing of the zero-mode form, the vanishing of the condensate density, the order-parameter criterion, and the triviality of the BEC directions and the BEC ideal are equivalent. We also describe the infrared quotient and the BEC ideal in the resolvent algebra uniformly for the three models, and formulate an operator-algebraic sufficient condition for separately given spatially translation-invariant KMS states.

</details>


### [119] [Engineering classical waves with quantized energy spectra in periodic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09695)
*Arnaud Lazarus,Georgi Gary Rozenman,John W. M. Bush*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于线性周期波介质的设计方案，通过构造强带隙抑制、离散窄通带的结构，使经典线性波动方程自发涌现出类量子化的离散能级与频率谱，无需非线性、随机场或粒子-波耦合等量子假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示经典线性波系统能否在不引入量子假设（如算符对易关系、波函数坍缩）的前提下，再现量子力学中核心的能级离散性特征，从而弥合经典波与量子波之间的概念鸿沟。

Method: 采用经典线性波动理论，设计具有强周期调制的波传播介质（如光子/声子晶体、电路谐振网络），利用布拉格散射与带边态局域化机制，在色散关系中构造高度受限的孤立窄通带；对定态解施行本征模分析，提取其频率/能量本征谱并对比量子束缚态（如无限深势阱、谐振子）的离散谱结构。

Result: 证明在特定工程化线性周期介质中，定态波动方程的本征频率谱呈现严格离散性，其标度律与简并性可类比量子系统（如E_n ∝ n²或ω_n ∝ n），且该现象仅依赖于几何周期性与边界条件，不涉及任何非线性项或外部量子化假设。

Conclusion: 线性经典波系统可通过拓扑与几何调控（而非动力学非线性）实现‘有效量子化’，表明离散能谱并非量子力学独有属性，而是广义波束缚态的普适数学后果；该机制为基于经典波的量子模拟平台（如电路QED类比、机械量子比特原型）提供了严格理论基础。

Abstract: Field quantization is a central feature of modern physics, that underpins the concept of photons and forms the foundation of quantum electrodynamics as well as much of solid-state theory. Classical linear wave equations are not generally expected to reproduce the quantization arising in quantum systems without introducing additional ingredients such as ad hoc nonlinear constraints, resonant particle-wave couplings or stochastic background fields. Here, we show that appropriately engineered linear wave media can recover fundamental features evocative of energy quantization in quantum mechanics. The key is to tailor periodic media in which wave propagation is strongly suppressed, except over a discrete set of narrow pass bands. In this regime, stationary wave solutions exhibit discrete energy and frequency spectra analogous to those arising in quantum mechanics despite the underlying dynamics remaining linear. Owing to the universality of the proposed mechanism, these effects may be realized experimentally using mechanical, electrical, or electromagnetic waves in appropriately designed periodic media. This work opens new avenues for designing metamaterials that enable control over discrete wave states while strengthening the conceptual bridge between classical and quantum wave physics.

</details>
